## 2026-06-10 Claude player storage bridge pass

- Closed the catalog player's biggest playfeel gap: best scores and settings now persist for games played inside the player. Sandboxed player iframes (`allow-scripts`, no `allow-same-origin`) run with opaque origins, so saves previously lived only in `workshop-runtime.js`'s in-memory fallback and vanished on every reload. The new storage bridge keeps the sandbox unchanged: `openPlayer()` seeds each game's saved entries through a `#wa-storage=` URL fragment (synchronous, so a game's first reads are already correct), the runtime batches writes back over `postMessage` per task tick, and a guarded catalog listener mirrors them into the catalog origin's localStorage under `workshop-arcade:game:<slug>:` keys. The runtime strips the seed fragment before game code runs and answers a hello with a fresh snapshot only while the game has not written yet.
- Hardened the parent listener: messages must arrive with origin `null` from the live player frame window, echo the active game's slug, and pass per-op shape checks (key <= 256 chars, value <= 32 KB, <= 64 ops per message) plus a 256 KB per-game mirror budget before touching storage. `clear()` removes only the active game's mirror keys, other games' mirrors and catalog shell keys are untouchable by construction, slugs must match `^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$` for the bridge to engage, and direct game loads keep native localStorage with the bridge fully dormant (no messages, no mirror writes). No game needed changes; the contract stays "use localStorage normally".
- Added `npm run test:player-storage-bridge` (TDD: the gate was written first and observed failing on all five behaviors) which drives the real catalog in Chromium and proves seeding, write-behind mirroring, scoped `clear()`, direct-load dormancy, and persistence across a full reload + player reopen, with zero console/page errors. Wired it as a Playwright-backed gate: `package.json` script, `run-fast-tests.mjs` exclusion with reason, `check-test-aggregator.mjs` and `check-docs-drift.mjs` allowlists, a `Run player storage bridge probe` step in the Validate Catalog `game-smoke` job, plus README and `docs/game-contract.md` documentation.
- The shared `websites/workshop-runtime.js` grew 1.7 KB -> 4.3 KB, which counts against every game's static page weight, so the Brick Breaker and Arcade Jump named-exception budgets moved 120 -> 125 KB and 110 -> 115 KB in `scripts/audit-pagespeed.mjs` (rationale comment updated; measured weights unchanged at 112.9 KB and 102.2 KB, restoring 12.1 KB / 12.8 KB named exception headroom). Refreshed the service-worker shell cache to `SHELL_REVISION = shell-86b1c679a1b8` / `VERSION = wa-v47-shell-86b1c679a1b8` and recorded performance baseline pass 108 (catalog local shell 172.1 KB / 200 KB, PWA install payload 176.2 KB / 200 KB, Catalog audit 162.4 KB / 6 requests).
- Verification passed: new bridge gate red-then-green; `npm test` (all 45 fast gates including pwa, page-weight, docs, performance-baseline, test-aggregator, security-workflows); `npm run test:player-storage-bridge`; `npm run test:runtime-storage`; `npm run test:pwa-runtime`; `npm run test:games` for 100 games; `npm run capture:games:ci` for 200 surfaces with max score 0 (one transient infrastructure abort on first attempt resolved on rerun, matching the known `ERR_NO_BUFFER_SPACE` class); `npm run audit:perf:local` across 101 pages with zero console/page errors; `git diff --check`.

## 2026-06-04 Claude Diamond Derby pass (78 -> 79 games)

- Added **Diamond Derby** as a compact swing-timing home-run derby for the sparse Sports category (3 -> 4 games), deliberately distinct from the three existing aim-and-power Sports games (Circuit Putt, Vector Pool, Penalty Circuit). A pitched ball plus a sweeping timing bar set the contact window; the player swings (Space/Enter/tap) for a Perfect/Good/Early/Late grade and tunes a launch angle (the middle angle carries farthest), with carry resolved through a projectile-style distance model.
